It has been a bit challenging to extract the features and application fields from the PDF catalogue you provided, but we're getting closer to a resolution.In the meantime, do you have any assets for your product images that you can share with us? We won't be able to extract the images from the catalogue directly.We'll be sure to provide you with an update once we finalize your product file and will be on the lookout for any assets/files you have related to the product images.Best, Kylor

## ✉️ TICKET #13861 [tier A] — 2026-01-20T16:59:27+00:00 — Re: MagicLite + SuperCat Onboarding - Next Steps & Action Items

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* magiclite.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* onboarding

```
Hi Jen &amp; Jen,Great to connect with you both today! Thanks for taking the time to walk through where we're at with the catalog setup. I know you're in the thick of onboarding new team members, so we really appreciate you making the time to meet with us. Here's a quick breakdown of what we'll each be tackling over the next week:SuperCat's Action Items:Send you the core files - I have attached the current files (Products &amp; Taxonomies) we discussed today, which we will refine once you provide us with any outlines or direct updates to the files attached. Image matching - Our developer will work on mapping your product images to the correct SKUs based on the naming pattern we identified (looking for matches like "LTS2", "SLID 30K", etc.)Pricing Data: We will work for merge and update your Product File to include the ML and NSL price lists you previously shared with us to add price levels in eCat. MagicLite's Action Items:Set up your eCat access - You have both been sent and accepted your eCat Admin login access which can be used to access your eCat admin instance for real-time visibility into the current state of your instance. Below is the link to view it via eCat Online which we have enabled during the onboarding process: https://supercat.supercatsolutions.com/mali/e/1/login?return_url=%2Fmali%2Fe%2F1%2FproductsReview the product &amp; taxonomy files - Let us know what needs to be edited, deleted, renamed, or reclassified. Feel free to send us any updated versions, or just send us feedback/instructions directly. Flag any missing images - We noticed we might be missing some (like LTS Pro bronze), so please review the current images that are live (via eOL link above or via the iPad app) and upload any new images that should be included. Prepare customer/rep exports - When you have bandwidth, pull your current Customer and Inventory data from Dynamics GP to be uploaded to eCat using the templates here:Customer FileInventory FileProduct Stories: Gather any data or files you have to support our Product Stories feature within eCat; see related Knowledge Base article here: Product StoriesOrder 2 iPads - For eCat testing (any model from the last 5+ years works fine)We'll target another check-in around this same time next week to review progress. Once we get through this foundation work, we can start having fun with smart lists, presentations, and rolling this out to your reps.Let me know if you have any questions in the meantime!Best,Kylor
```

## ✉️ TICKET #13861 [tier A] — 2026-02-13T18:13:53+00:00 — Re: MagicLite + SuperCat Onboarding - Next Steps & Action Items

*From:* kylor@supercatsolutions.com (`user`) · *customer domain:* magiclite.com · *status:* closed · *tags:* onboarding

```
Hi Jen, I hope you've had a great week. See below for my answers to your initial questions on the product file (in bold): Do all columns need to be filled in? For example, some categories just don't have that many layers - is it ok to leave some spots empty?Do all columns need to be filled in?No. Only the columns marked with a "Y" in the "Req?" (Required) column of the Product File Guide must be filled. Optional fields (like MediumDesc or Materials) can be left blank.Can I leave category "layers" empty?No. The product file requires three specific classification layers for every item, and they cannot be left blank:TradeNameCode (Required)CollectionCode (Required)CategoryCode (Required)If a product doesn't naturally have a collection or specific tradename, you should create a generic code (e.g., GEN or STD) to fill those spots in the file.For Applications - is this a category style column? For example, is this where we list characteristics like "outdoor; RGB; CCT" etc.? Will these allow products to be filtered by later?Yes, this&nbsp;can&nbsp;absolutely&nbsp;be&nbsp;used&nbsp;as a filterable column&nbsp;where&nbsp;you'd&nbsp;list&nbsp;characteristics&nbsp;like "outdoor", "RGB", "CCT", etc.How&nbsp;it works:In&nbsp;the&nbsp;product&nbsp;file&nbsp;- You&nbsp;can use&nbsp;comma-separated values to&nbsp;tag&nbsp;a&nbsp;product with multiple attributes. For example:"outdoor, RGB"&nbsp;- product&nbsp;appears&nbsp;under both filter&nbsp;options"indoor,&nbsp;CCT"&nbsp;- product appears under&nbsp;both filter options"outdoor"&nbsp;- product only&nbsp;appears under one&nbsp;filterIn Admin&nbsp;Console&nbsp;- The&nbsp;"Application" field needs&nbsp;to be configured as a&nbsp;multi-select filter&nbsp;under&nbsp;Products&nbsp;&gt; Custom&nbsp;Fields. Once&nbsp;set up, every&nbsp;unique&nbsp;value you&nbsp;use&nbsp;becomes&nbsp;a filter option in&nbsp;eCat.Tips&nbsp;for best&nbsp;results:Keep&nbsp;values consistent (same spelling and&nbsp;capitalization) so they group properlyLimit&nbsp;multi-select filters&nbsp;to 10-15 choices&nbsp;for&nbsp;the best&nbsp;user&nbsp;experienceMake&nbsp;sure&nbsp;"Send&nbsp;to iPad" is checked for&nbsp;the&nbsp;field&nbsp;to be&nbsp;filterableSo&nbsp;to&nbsp;answer your question directly: yes, this&nbsp;is&nbsp;exactly&nbsp;where&nbsp;you'd list&nbsp;those&nbsp;product&nbsp;characteristics, and yes, they'll&nbsp;be filterable once the&nbsp;field is configured properly in admin.For the Related Products column: Are we to list the Parent or the Accessories in that column. For example: Is SL Tape listed in the accessory (like a power feed) row, or vice versa? Or both? My gut says to list accessories in the tape column, in the hope that those would be listed on the tape page to make life simple for clients looking for accessories. Let us know what your preferred approach is.Great&nbsp;question!&nbsp;The recommended approach is actually&nbsp;both directions.This works best if BaseItemCodes are included for ALL the items in the list for each&nbsp;item that's related.Recommended approach:
  
BaseItemCodeRelatedItemsSL-Tape-001SL-Tape-001, SL-PowerFeed-001, SL-Jumper-001, SL-Splice-001SL-PowerFeed-001SL-Tape-001, SL-PowerFeed-001, SL-Jumper-001, SL-Splice-001SL-Jumper-001SL-Tape-001, SL-PowerFeed-001, SL-Jumper-001, SL-Splice-001SL-Splice-001SL-Tape-001, SL-PowerFeed-001, SL-Jumper-001, SL-Splice-001Why both directions?When&nbsp;a customer views the tape, they see&nbsp;all compatible accessoriesWhen a customer&nbsp;views an accessory (maybe from search), they can easily find the parent tape&nbsp;it goes withCreates a seamless browsing experience no matter where they&nbsp;landPro tip:&nbsp;Products display&nbsp;in the Related Items list in the order&nbsp;you list them. So you could list&nbsp;the main tape first, then accessories&nbsp;- that way the "parent" always appears&nbsp;at the top.Let me know if this helps or if you have any additional questions!Have a great weekend, Kylor
```

```
Hi Team, We've&nbsp;completed the initial pricing setup in eCat using the ML and NSL price&nbsp;lists you provided. Here's a quick&nbsp;summary of what was done and where we&nbsp;need your help.What We&nbsp;Set UpWe configured&nbsp;4 price levels in the admin console and&nbsp;added them to your product file:ML List&nbsp;(mllist) — List price from the ML price&nbsp;listML DN&nbsp;(mldn) — DN price from the&nbsp;ML price listNSL&nbsp;List&nbsp;(nsllist)&nbsp;— List price from the NSL price listNSL DN&nbsp;(nsldn) — DN price from the&nbsp;NSL price list262 products&nbsp;from your catalog were matched and now have&nbsp;pricing populated across those four levels.Heads Up on Recent Product UploadI noticed you've been doing&nbsp;some uploads and updates on your end&nbsp;— great to see! Just a heads up&nbsp;though, the most recent product file upload&nbsp;dropped the 5 custom columns I&nbsp;added previously to support the hierarchy we discussed:ProductTypeProductFamilyApplicationSubTypeRelatedItemsI've&nbsp;attached an updated product file with those fields&nbsp;added back in along with the new pricing&nbsp;columns, so you should be good to&nbsp;use this as your base going forward.I've also added these as custom&nbsp;fields in the admin console, along&nbsp;with Certifications and UPC. You can review, edit, or reorder all&nbsp;of them under&nbsp;Products &gt; Custom Fields. Note that ProductType is a&nbsp;standard field already built into eCat, which is why it won't show&nbsp;up there.Import&nbsp;Errors to&nbsp;ReviewThere&nbsp;are still&nbsp;some&nbsp;warnings&nbsp;on&nbsp;the current&nbsp;import&nbsp;that&nbsp;you&nbsp;can&nbsp;review under&nbsp;Tools&nbsp;&gt; Admin&nbsp;Reports&nbsp;&gt; File&nbsp;Import&nbsp;Status. Two&nbsp;things&nbsp;to be&nbsp;aware of:Duplicate BaseItemCodes&nbsp;— Several&nbsp;products&nbsp;(e.g., SL-OD-MC, VO-C10-8, NFLX-ENDS, NFLX-CLIPS, MGEC, ACC-12G-2C-BUR, and&nbsp;others) appear&nbsp;on&nbsp;multiple rows in the product file.&nbsp;eCat requires&nbsp;each BaseItemCode to be unique&nbsp;— when&nbsp;it finds duplicates, it only keeps&nbsp;the last occurrence and&nbsp;discards the earlier ones. This is&nbsp;likely happening because the same accessory or&nbsp;component is&nbsp;listed under&nbsp;multiple product families instead&nbsp;of once with multiple CollectionCodes. These&nbsp;should&nbsp;be consolidated&nbsp;to&nbsp;a&nbsp;single row per&nbsp;item.Image limit exceeded&nbsp;— Three&nbsp;items&nbsp;(FL-15-CT, FL-30-CT, FL-50-CT) have more than&nbsp;6 images listed. eCat caps at 6 per&nbsp;product, so the&nbsp;extras&nbsp;were dropped.Where We Need ClarificationThere are approximately 685&nbsp;products in your product file that don't&nbsp;appear in either the ML or NSL&nbsp;price lists. These products do have a&nbsp;"Net Price" value already — for&nbsp;example, the Streamline tape series, Standard tape accessories, connectors, power supplies, etc.A few&nbsp;questions:How should we&nbsp;handle the Net Price for these 685 products? Should it map to one&nbsp;of the four price levels above, or&nbsp;is it a different price tier?Do these products apply to both&nbsp;ML and NSL, or only one&nbsp;brand? This determines which price columns to&nbsp;populate.We also noticed&nbsp;25 items in the price lists&nbsp;that aren't in the current product file&nbsp;— likely newer additions or discontinued SKUs. Thanks a ton for the updates! As it relates to "Application" this was in reference to an initial image Jen shared with me below. And we proposed using a Custom Filed (Application) for the Third Column (Indoor/Outdoor/Single Colour/RGB/CCT)The&nbsp;Related Items&nbsp;field lets&nbsp;you link&nbsp;products&nbsp;together&nbsp;so&nbsp;that&nbsp;when a&nbsp;customer views one&nbsp;item, they'll&nbsp;automatically&nbsp;see suggested&nbsp;companion&nbsp;products. For&nbsp;example, if someone&nbsp;is&nbsp;looking&nbsp;at a dining&nbsp;table, Related&nbsp;Items could surface&nbsp;the&nbsp;matching&nbsp;chairs, a&nbsp;table&nbsp;pad, or&nbsp;a&nbsp;complementary buffet.How it&nbsp;works:In&nbsp;your&nbsp;product file, the&nbsp;RelatedItems&nbsp;field accepts&nbsp;a comma-separated list of item&nbsp;codes&nbsp;(e.g.,&nbsp;ABC-100,ABC-200,ABC-300)Those&nbsp;linked&nbsp;items will&nbsp;appear as&nbsp;recommendations&nbsp;on&nbsp;the product&nbsp;detail&nbsp;page in&nbsp;the appIt's a&nbsp;great way to drive&nbsp;cross-sells, highlight&nbsp;collections, or connect&nbsp;accessories&nbsp;to main&nbsp;productsBest&nbsp;use&nbsp;cases:Grouping items&nbsp;in&nbsp;the same&nbsp;collection&nbsp;or&nbsp;familyLinking&nbsp;accessories&nbsp;or&nbsp;add-ons to a&nbsp;main productSuggesting&nbsp;complementary pieces&nbsp;(e.g., a lamp&nbsp;with a matching&nbsp;shade)You&nbsp;control&nbsp;exactly&nbsp;which&nbsp;items appear&nbsp;— just add or&nbsp;remove item&nbsp;codes from the field&nbsp;in&nbsp;your product CSV&nbsp;and&nbsp;re-import.Let me know if that helps.
```

```
Hi All,Quick follow-up on both the product and customer files.**Product File**We ran into a few things with the file you sent back — some columns were in the wrong order, a chunk of products were missing, and the price level columns got dropped. No worries, we sorted it out on our end. We rebuilt from the last clean import, fixed the structure, and merged duplicates where needed. The cleaned file (906 products) is imported and live.There are 4 products that had conflicting info across duplicate rows (different prices, descriptions, etc.) — we went with the most recent values but it'd be good for someone on your side to give those a quick look. We moved those over to the BuyerEmail column so they're preserved, but those rows will still need real street addresses added when you get a chance.There are also some other gaps in the NSL data — missing street addresses, city/state/zip, and country codes on a good number of rows. Nothing unusual for a first pass with a list this size.We went ahead and uploaded everything as-is. The records with complete data will come through fine, and the ones with gaps will just flag in the import error report so they can be cleaned up in a future update. No rush on any of it.**Weekly Standup**We'd love to get the remaining data items buttoned up by end of month. Team, 
```

```
I've completed extensive updates to the Magic Lite eCat catalog and would like you to review everything on your iPad app before our meeting tomorrow.

What's been updated:
• 694 products across 5 collections (Linear Lighting, Under Cabinet, Down Lighting, Landscape, Industrial)
• Product images - all products now have images with correct product photos as the primary image
• Pricing - NetPrice and custom price levels (ML/NSL) populated for all applicable products
• Product stories - descriptions added for all products
• Inventory - merged NSL and ML warehouse data

Before our meeting, please:
1. Open the eCat app on your iPad
2. Tap the gear icon and refresh data (critical - pulls the latest updates)
3. Browse through each collection and spot-check:

We can use tomorrow's call to review everything live, discuss any adjustments needed, and cover app layout/configuration changes.

Looking forward to the review.

Kylor
```
